[Grammy Award for Best Children's Music Album](/wiki/Grammy_Award_for_Best_Children%27s_Music_Album "Grammy Award for Best Children's Music Album")  
---  
1959−1975| 

  * "[The Chipmunk Song (Christmas Don't Be Late)](/wiki/The_Chipmunk_Song_\(Christmas_Don%27t_Be_Late\) "The Chipmunk Song \(Christmas Don't Be Late\)")" – [Ross Bagdasarian Sr.](/wiki/Ross_Bagdasarian "Ross Bagdasarian") (1959)
  * _[Peter and the Wolf](/wiki/Peter_and_the_Wolf "Peter and the Wolf")_ – [Peter Ustinov](/wiki/Peter_Ustinov "Peter Ustinov") (1960)
  * _[Let's All Sing with The Chipmunks](/wiki/Let%27s_All_Sing_with_The_Chipmunks "Let's All Sing with The Chipmunks")_ – [Ross Bagdasarian Sr.](/wiki/Ross_Bagdasarian "Ross Bagdasarian") (1961)
  * _[Prokofiev: Peter and The Wolf](/wiki/Peter_and_the_Wolf "Peter and the Wolf")_ – [Leonard Bernstein](/wiki/Leonard_Bernstein "Leonard Bernstein") (1962)
  * _[Saint-Saëns: Carnival of the Animals](/wiki/Carnival_of_the_Animals "Carnival of the Animals")_ /_[Britten: Young Person's Guide to the Orchestra](/wiki/Young_Person%27s_Guide_to_the_Orchestra "Young Person's Guide to the Orchestra")_ – [Leonard Bernstein](/wiki/Leonard_Bernstein "Leonard Bernstein") (1963)
  * _Bernstein Conducts for Young People_ – [Leonard Bernstein](/wiki/Leonard_Bernstein "Leonard Bernstein") (1964)
  * _[Mary Poppins: Original Cast Soundtrack](/wiki/Mary_Poppins:_Original_Cast_Soundtrack "Mary Poppins: Original Cast Soundtrack")_ – [Dick Van Dyke](/wiki/Dick_Van_Dyke "Dick Van Dyke") & [Julie Andrews](/wiki/Julie_Andrews "Julie Andrews") (1965)
  * _[Dr. Seuss](/wiki/Dr._Seuss "Dr. Seuss") Presents: "Fox in Sox" and "[Green Eggs and Ham](/wiki/Green_Eggs_and_Ham "Green Eggs and Ham")"_ – [Marvin Miller](/wiki/Marvin_Miller_\(actor\) "Marvin Miller \(actor\)") (1966)
  * _Dr. Seuss Presents - "If I Ran the Zoo" and "Sleep Book"_ – [Marvin Miller](/wiki/Marvin_Miller_\(actor\) "Marvin Miller \(actor\)") (1967)
  * _[Dr. Seuss: How The Grinch Stole Christmas](/wiki/How_the_Grinch_Stole_Christmas!_\(TV_special\) "How the Grinch Stole Christmas! \(TV special\)")_ – [Boris Karloff](/wiki/Boris_Karloff "Boris Karloff") (1968)
  * _[Peter, Paul and Mommy](/wiki/Peter,_Paul_and_Mommy "Peter, Paul and Mommy")_ – [Peter, Paul and Mary](/wiki/Peter,_Paul_and_Mary "Peter, Paul and Mary") (1970)
  * _[Sesame Street](/wiki/Sesame_Street "Sesame Street")_ – [Joan Cooney](/wiki/Joan_Ganz_Cooney "Joan Ganz Cooney") & [Thomas Z. Shepard](/wiki/Thomas_Z._Shepard "Thomas Z. Shepard") (producers) (1971)
  * _[Bill Cosby Talks to Kids About Drugs](/wiki/Bill_Cosby_Talks_to_Kids_About_Drugs "Bill Cosby Talks to Kids About Drugs")_ – [Bill Cosby](/wiki/Bill_Cosby "Bill Cosby") (1972)
  * _[The Electric Company](/wiki/The_Electric_Company "The Electric Company")_ – [Bill Cosby](/wiki/Bill_Cosby "Bill Cosby") & [Rita Moreno](/wiki/Rita_Moreno "Rita Moreno") (1973)
  * _[Sesame Street Live!](/wiki/Sesame_Street_discography "Sesame Street discography")_ – [Joe Raposo](/wiki/Joe_Raposo "Joe Raposo") (producer) (1974)
  * _[Winnie the Pooh and Tigger Too](/wiki/Winnie_the_Pooh_and_Tigger_Too "Winnie the Pooh and Tigger Too")_ – [Paul Winchell](/wiki/Paul_Winchell "Paul Winchell"), [Sebastian Cabot](/wiki/Sebastian_Cabot_\(actor\) "Sebastian Cabot \(actor\)") & [Sterling Holloway](/wiki/Sterling_Holloway "Sterling Holloway") (1975)

  
1976−1993| 

  * _The Little Prince_ – [Richard Burton](/wiki/Richard_Burton "Richard Burton") (1976)
  * [Prokofiev](/wiki/Sergei_Prokofiev "Sergei Prokofiev"): _[Peter and the Wolf](/wiki/Peter_and_the_Wolf "Peter and the Wolf")_ / [Saint-Saëns](/wiki/Camille_Saint-Sa%C3%ABns "Camille Saint-Saëns"): "[Carnival of the Animals](/wiki/Carnival_of_the_Animals "Carnival of the Animals")" – [Hermione Gingold](/wiki/Hermione_Gingold "Hermione Gingold") & [Karl Böhm](/wiki/Karl_B%C3%B6hm "Karl Böhm") (1977)
  * _[Aren't You Glad You're You](/wiki/Aren%27t_You_Glad_You%27re_You%3F_\(album\) "Aren't You Glad You're You? \(album\)")_ – [Christopher Cerf](/wiki/Christopher_Cerf_\(producer\) "Christopher Cerf \(producer\)") & Jim Timmens (producers) (1978)
  * _[The Muppet Show](/wiki/The_Muppet_Show_\(album\) "The Muppet Show \(album\)")_ – [Jim Henson](/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson") (producer) (1979)
  * _[The Muppet Movie](/wiki/The_Muppet_Movie_\(soundtrack\) "The Muppet Movie \(soundtrack\)")_ – [Jim Henson](/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson") & [Paul Williams](/wiki/Paul_Williams_\(songwriter\) "Paul Williams \(songwriter\)") (producers) (1980)
  * _[In Harmony: A Sesame Street Record](/wiki/In_Harmony_\(compilation_albums\) "In Harmony \(compilation albums\)")_ – [David Levine](/wiki/David_Levine "David Levine") & [Lucy Simon](/wiki/Lucy_Simon "Lucy Simon") (producers) (1981)
  * _[Sesame Country](/wiki/Sesame_Street_discography "Sesame Street discography")_ – [Jim Henson](/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson") & Dennis Scott (producers) (1982)
  * _[In Harmony 2](/wiki/In_Harmony_\(compilation_albums\) "In Harmony \(compilation albums\)")_ – David Levine & [Lucy Simon](/wiki/Lucy_Simon "Lucy Simon") (producers) (1983)
  * _[E.T. The Extra-Terrestrial](/wiki/E.T._the_Extra-Terrestrial_\(album\) "E.T. the Extra-Terrestrial \(album\)")_ – Michael Jackson and [Quincy Jones](/wiki/Quincy_Jones "Quincy Jones") (producer) (1984)
  * _[Where the Sidewalk Ends](/wiki/Where_the_Sidewalk_Ends "Where the Sidewalk Ends")_ – [Shel Silverstein](/wiki/Shel_Silverstein "Shel Silverstein") and Ron Haffkine (producer) (1985)
  * _[Sesame Street Presents: Follow That Bird](/wiki/Sesame_Street_Presents:_Follow_That_Bird "Sesame Street Presents: Follow That Bird")_ – [Jim Henson](/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson") & [Steve Buckingham](/wiki/Steve_Buckingham_\(record_producer\) "Steve Buckingham \(record producer\)") (producers) (1986)
  * _[The Alphabet](/wiki/Sesame_Street_discography "Sesame Street discography")_ – [Jim Henson](/wiki/Jim_Henson "Jim Henson"), Geri Van Rees & Kathryn King (producers) (1987)
  * _[The Elephant's Child](/wiki/Just_So_Stories "Just So Stories")_ – [Bobby McFerrin](/wiki/Bobby_McFerrin "Bobby McFerrin") & [Jack Nicholson](/wiki/Jack_Nicholson "Jack Nicholson") (1988)
  * _[Pecos Bill](/wiki/Pecos_Bill "Pecos Bill")_ – [Robin Williams](/wiki/Robin_Williams "Robin Williams") and [Ry Cooder](/wiki/Ry_Cooder "Ry Cooder") (1989)
  * _The Rock-A-Bye Collection, Volume 1_ – Tanya Goodman (1990)
  * _[The Little Mermaid: An Original Walt Disney Records Soundtrack](/wiki/The_Little_Mermaid_\(1989_soundtrack\) "The Little Mermaid \(1989 soundtrack\)")_ – [Alan Menken](/wiki/Alan_Menken "Alan Menken") & [Howard Ashman](/wiki/Howard_Ashman "Howard Ashman") (songwriters) (1991)
  * _A Cappella Kids_ – Clifford "Barney" Robertson (producer) (1992)
  * _[Beauty and the Beast: Original Motion Picture Soundtrack](/wiki/Beauty_and_the_Beast_\(1991_soundtrack\) "Beauty and the Beast \(1991 soundtrack\)")_ – [Alan Menken](/wiki/Alan_Menken "Alan Menken") & [Howard Ashman](/wiki/Howard_Ashman "Howard Ashman") (songwriters) (1993)

  
2012−present| 

  * _All About Bullies... Big and Small_ – Gloria Domina, James Cravero, Kevin Mackie, Patrick Robinson & Steve Pullara (producers) (2012)
  * _Can You Canoe?_ – The Okee Dokee Brothers (2013)
  * _Throw a Penny in the Wishing Well_ – [Jennifer Gasoi](/wiki/Jennifer_Gasoi "Jennifer Gasoi") (2014)
  * _[I Am Malala: How One Girl Stood Up for Education and Changed the World](/wiki/I_Am_Malala "I Am Malala")_ – Neela Vaswani (2015)
  * _Home_ – [Tim Kubart](/wiki/Tim_Kubart "Tim Kubart") (2016)
  * _Infinity Plus One_ – [Secret Agent 23 Skidoo](/wiki/Secret_Agent_23_Skidoo "Secret Agent 23 Skidoo") (2017)
  * _[Feel What U Feel](/wiki/Feel_What_U_Feel "Feel What U Feel")_ – [Lisa Loeb](/wiki/Lisa_Loeb "Lisa Loeb") (2018)
  * _All the Sounds_ – Lucy Kalantari & The Jazz Cats (2019)
  * _Ageless Songs for the Child Archetype_ – Jon Samson (2020)
  * _All the Ladies_ – [Joanie Leeds](/wiki/Joanie_Leeds "Joanie Leeds") (2021)
  * _A Colorful World_ − [Falu](/wiki/Falu "Falu") (2022)
  * _The Movement_ – Alphabet Rockers (2023)
  * _We Grow Together Preschool Songs_ – [123 Andrés](/wiki/123_Andr%C3%A9s "123 Andrés") (2024)
  * _Brillo, Brillo!_ – [Lucky Diaz and the Family Jam Band](/wiki/Lucky_Diaz_and_the_Family_Jam_Band "Lucky Diaz and the Family Jam Band") (2025)
  * _Harmony_ − [FYÜTCH & Aura V](/wiki/Fy%C3%BCtch "Fyütch") (2026)

  
From 1994–2011, the category was split into [Best Musical Album for Children](/wiki/Grammy_Award_for_Best_Musical_Album_for_Children "Grammy Award for Best Musical Album for Children") and [Best Spoken Word Album for Children](/wiki/Grammy_Award_for_Best_Spoken_Word_Album_for_Children "Grammy Award for Best Spoken Word Album for Children").

  * "[Lollipops and Roses](/wiki/Lollipops_and_Roses_\(song\) "Lollipops and Roses \(song\)")" – [Jack Jones](/wiki/Jack_Jones_\(American_singer\) "Jack Jones \(American singer\)") (1962)

  * "[By the Time I Get to Phoenix](/wiki/By_the_Time_I_Get_to_Phoenix "By the Time I Get to Phoenix")" – [Glen Campbell](/wiki/Glen_Campbell "Glen Campbell") (1968)

1970s| 

  * "[Everybody's Talkin'](/wiki/Everybody%27s_Talkin%27 "Everybody's Talkin'")" – [Harry Nilsson](/wiki/Harry_Nilsson "Harry Nilsson") (1970)
  * "[Everything Is Beautiful](/wiki/Everything_Is_Beautiful "Everything Is Beautiful")" – [Ray Stevens](/wiki/Ray_Stevens "Ray Stevens") (1971)

  * "[You Are the Sunshine of My Life](/wiki/You_Are_the_Sunshine_of_My_Life "You Are the Sunshine of My Life")" – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1974)
  * _[Fulfillingness' First Finale](/wiki/Fulfillingness%27_First_Finale "Fulfillingness' First Finale")_ – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1975)
  * _[Still Crazy After All These Years](/wiki/Still_Crazy_After_All_These_Years "Still Crazy After All These Years")_ – [Paul Simon](/wiki/Paul_Simon "Paul Simon") (1976)
  * _[Songs in the Key of Life](/wiki/Songs_in_the_Key_of_Life "Songs in the Key of Life")_ – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1977)
  * "[Handy Man](/wiki/Handy_Man_\(song\) "Handy Man \(song\)")" – [James Taylor](/wiki/James_Taylor "James Taylor") (1978)

[Grammy Award for Best R&B Song](/wiki/Grammy_Award_for_Best_R%26B_Song "Grammy Award for Best R&B Song")  
---  
Awarded to songwriters  
1960s| 

  * "[(Sittin' On) The Dock of the Bay](/wiki/\(Sittin%27_On\)_The_Dock_of_the_Bay "\(Sittin' On\) The Dock of the Bay")" – [Otis Redding](/wiki/Otis_Redding "Otis Redding") & [Steve Cropper](/wiki/Steve_Cropper "Steve Cropper") (1969)

  
1970s| 

  * "[Color Him Father](/wiki/Color_Him_Father "Color Him Father")" – [Richard Lewis Spencer](/wiki/Richard_Lewis_Spencer "Richard Lewis Spencer") (1970)
  * "[Patches](/wiki/Patches_\(Chairmen_of_the_Board_song\) "Patches \(Chairmen of the Board song\)")" – [Ronald Dunbar](/wiki/Ron_Dunbar "Ron Dunbar") & [General Johnson](/wiki/General_Johnson_\(musician\) "General Johnson \(musician\)") (1971)
  * "[Ain't No Sunshine](/wiki/Ain%27t_No_Sunshine "Ain't No Sunshine")" – [Bill Withers](/wiki/Bill_Withers "Bill Withers") (1972)
  * "[Papa Was a Rollin' Stone](/wiki/Papa_Was_a_Rollin%27_Stone "Papa Was a Rollin' Stone")" – [Barrett Strong](/wiki/Barrett_Strong "Barrett Strong") & [Norman Whitfield](/wiki/Norman_Whitfield "Norman Whitfield") (1973)
  * "[Superstition](/wiki/Superstition_\(song\) "Superstition \(song\)")" – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1974)
  * "[Living for the City](/wiki/Living_for_the_City "Living for the City")" – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1975)
  * "Where Is the Love" – [Harry Wayne Casey](/wiki/Harry_Wayne_Casey "Harry Wayne Casey"), Willie Clarke, [Richard Finch](/wiki/Richard_Finch_\(producer\) "Richard Finch \(producer\)") & [Betty Wright](/wiki/Betty_Wright "Betty Wright") (1976)
  * "[Lowdown](/wiki/Lowdown_\(Boz_Scaggs_song\) "Lowdown \(Boz Scaggs song\)")" – [Boz Scaggs](/wiki/Boz_Scaggs "Boz Scaggs") & [David Paich](/wiki/David_Paich "David Paich") (1977)
  * "[You Make Me Feel Like Dancing](/wiki/You_Make_Me_Feel_Like_Dancing "You Make Me Feel Like Dancing")" – [Leo Sayer](/wiki/Leo_Sayer "Leo Sayer") & [Vini Poncia](/wiki/Vini_Poncia "Vini Poncia") (1978)
  * "[Last Dance](/wiki/Last_Dance_\(Donna_Summer_song\) "Last Dance \(Donna Summer song\)")" – [Paul Jabara](/wiki/Paul_Jabara "Paul Jabara") (1979)

  
1980s| 

  * "[After the Love Has Gone](/wiki/After_the_Love_Has_Gone "After the Love Has Gone")" – [David Foster](/wiki/David_Foster "David Foster"), [Jay Graydon](/wiki/Jay_Graydon "Jay Graydon") & [Bill Champlin](/wiki/Bill_Champlin "Bill Champlin") (1980)
  * "[Never Knew Love Like This Before](/wiki/Never_Knew_Love_Like_This_Before "Never Knew Love Like This Before")" – [Reggie Lucas](/wiki/Reggie_Lucas "Reggie Lucas") & [James Mtume](/wiki/James_Mtume "James Mtume") (1981)
  * "[Just the Two of Us](/wiki/Just_the_Two_of_Us_\(Grover_Washington_Jr._song\) "Just the Two of Us \(Grover Washington Jr. song\)")" – [Bill Withers](/wiki/Bill_Withers "Bill Withers"), William Salter & [Ralph MacDonald](/wiki/Ralph_MacDonald "Ralph MacDonald") (1982)
  * "[Turn Your Love Around](/wiki/Turn_Your_Love_Around "Turn Your Love Around")" – [Jay Graydon](/wiki/Jay_Graydon "Jay Graydon"), [Steve Lukather](/wiki/Steve_Lukather "Steve Lukather") & [Bill Champlin](/wiki/Bill_Champlin "Bill Champlin") (1983)
  * "[Billie Jean](/wiki/Billie_Jean "Billie Jean")" – Michael Jackson (1984)
  * "[I Feel for You](/wiki/I_Feel_for_You "I Feel for You")" – [Prince](/wiki/Prince_\(musician\) "Prince \(musician\)") (1985)
  * "[Freeway of Love](/wiki/Freeway_of_Love "Freeway of Love")" – [Jeffrey E. Cohen](/wiki/Jeffrey_E._Cohen "Jeffrey E. Cohen") & [Narada Michael Walden](/wiki/Narada_Michael_Walden "Narada Michael Walden") (1986)
  * "[Sweet Love](/wiki/Sweet_Love_\(Anita_Baker_song\) "Sweet Love \(Anita Baker song\)")" – [Anita Baker](/wiki/Anita_Baker "Anita Baker"), Louis A. Johnson & [Gary Bias](/wiki/Gary_Bias "Gary Bias") (1987)
  * "[Lean on Me](/wiki/Lean_on_Me_\(song\) "Lean on Me \(song\)")" – [Bill Withers](/wiki/Bill_Withers "Bill Withers") (1988)
  * "[Giving You the Best That I Got](/wiki/Giving_You_the_Best_That_I_Got_\(song\) "Giving You the Best That I Got \(song\)")" – [Anita Baker](/wiki/Anita_Baker "Anita Baker"), Randy Holland & [Skip Scarborough](/wiki/Skip_Scarborough "Skip Scarborough") (1989)

  
1990s| 

  * "[If You Don't Know Me by Now](/wiki/If_You_Don%27t_Know_Me_by_Now "If You Don't Know Me by Now")" – [Kenny Gamble and Leon Huff](/wiki/Gamble_and_Huff "Gamble and Huff") (1990)
  * "[U Can't Touch This](/wiki/U_Can%27t_Touch_This "U Can't Touch This")" – [MC Hammer](/wiki/MC_Hammer "MC Hammer"), [Rick James](/wiki/Rick_James "Rick James") & Alonzo Miller (1991)
  * "[Power of Love/Love Power](/wiki/Power_of_Love/Love_Power "Power of Love/Love Power")" – [Marcus Miller](/wiki/Marcus_Miller "Marcus Miller"), [Luther Vandross](/wiki/Luther_Vandross "Luther Vandross") & Teddy Vann (1992)
  * "[End of the Road](/wiki/End_of_the_Road_\(Boyz_II_Men_song\) "End of the Road \(Boyz II Men song\)")" – [Babyface](/wiki/Babyface_\(musician\) "Babyface \(musician\)"), [L.A. Reid](/wiki/L.A._Reid "L.A. Reid") & [Daryl Simmons](/wiki/Daryl_Simmons "Daryl Simmons") (1993)
  * "[That's the Way Love Goes](/wiki/That%27s_the_Way_Love_Goes_\(Janet_Jackson_song\) "That's the Way Love Goes \(Janet Jackson song\)")" – [Janet Jackson](/wiki/Janet_Jackson "Janet Jackson"), [ Jimmy Jam & Terry Lewis](/wiki/Jimmy_Jam_and_Terry_Lewis "Jimmy Jam and Terry Lewis") (1994)
  * "[I'll Make Love to You](/wiki/I%27ll_Make_Love_to_You "I'll Make Love to You")" – [Babyface](/wiki/Babyface_\(musician\) "Babyface \(musician\)") (1995)
  * "[For Your Love](/wiki/For_Your_Love_\(Stevie_Wonder_song\) "For Your Love \(Stevie Wonder song\)")" – [Stevie Wonder](/wiki/Stevie_Wonder "Stevie Wonder") (1996)
  * "[Exhale (Shoop Shoop)](/wiki/Exhale_\(Shoop_Shoop\) "Exhale \(Shoop Shoop\)")" – [Babyface](/wiki/Babyface_\(musician\) "Babyface \(musician\)") (1997)
  * "[I Believe I Can Fly](/wiki/I_Believe_I_Can_Fly "I Believe I Can Fly")" – [R. Kelly](/wiki/R._Kelly "R. Kelly") (1998)
  * "[Doo Wop (That Thing)](/wiki/Doo_Wop_\(That_Thing\) "Doo Wop \(That Thing\)")" – [Lauryn Hill](/wiki/Lauryn_Hill "Lauryn Hill") (1999)

  
2000s| 

  * "[No Scrubs](/wiki/No_Scrubs "No Scrubs")" – [Kandi Burruss](/wiki/Kandi_Burruss "Kandi Burruss"), [Kevin Briggs](/wiki/Kevin_Briggs_\(music_producer\) "Kevin Briggs \(music producer\)") & [Tameka Cottle](/wiki/Tameka_Cottle "Tameka Cottle") (2000)
  * "[Say My Name](/wiki/Say_My_Name "Say My Name")" – [Beyoncé](/wiki/Beyonc%C3%A9 "Beyoncé"), [Rodney Jerkins](/wiki/Rodney_Jerkins "Rodney Jerkins"), [LaShawn Daniels](/wiki/LaShawn_Daniels "LaShawn Daniels"), [Fred Jerkins III](/wiki/Fred_Jerkins_III "Fred Jerkins III"), [LeToya Luckett](/wiki/LeToya_Luckett "LeToya Luckett"), [LaTavia Roberson](/wiki/LaTavia_Roberson "LaTavia Roberson") & [Kelly Rowland](/wiki/Kelly_Rowland "Kelly Rowland") (2001)
  * "[Fallin'](/wiki/Fallin%27_\(Alicia_Keys_song\) "Fallin' \(Alicia Keys song\)")" – [Alicia Keys](/wiki/Alicia_Keys "Alicia Keys") (2002)
  * "[Love of My Life (An Ode to Hip-Hop)](/wiki/Love_of_My_Life_\(An_Ode_to_Hip-Hop\) "Love of My Life \(An Ode to Hip-Hop\)")" – [Erykah Badu](/wiki/Erykah_Badu "Erykah Badu"), Glenn Standridge, [James Poyser](/wiki/James_Poyser "James Poyser"), Madukwu Chinwah, [Raphael Saadiq](/wiki/Raphael_Saadiq "Raphael Saadiq"), [Lonnie Lynn](/wiki/Common_\(rapper\) "Common \(rapper\)") & Robert Ozuna (2003)
  * "[Crazy in Love](/wiki/Crazy_in_Love "Crazy in Love")" – [Beyoncé Knowles](/wiki/Beyonc%C3%A9 "Beyoncé"), [Shawn Carter](/wiki/Jay-Z "Jay-Z") & [Rich Harrison](/wiki/Rich_Harrison "Rich Harrison") (2004)
  * "[You Don't Know My Name](/wiki/You_Don%27t_Know_My_Name "You Don't Know My Name")" – [Alicia Keys](/wiki/Alicia_Keys "Alicia Keys"), [Harold Lilly](/wiki/Harold_Lilly_\(songwriter\) "Harold Lilly \(songwriter\)") & [Kanye West](/wiki/Kanye_West "Kanye West") (2005)
  * "[We Belong Together](/wiki/We_Belong_Together "We Belong Together")" – [Mariah Carey](/wiki/Mariah_Carey "Mariah Carey"), [Johntá Austin](/wiki/Johnt%C3%A1_Austin "Johntá Austin"), [Manuel Seal Jr.](/wiki/Manuel_Seal "Manuel Seal") & [Jermaine Dupri](/wiki/Jermaine_Dupri "Jermaine Dupri") (2006)
  * "[Be Without You](/wiki/Be_Without_You "Be Without You")" – [Bryan-Michael Cox](/wiki/Bryan-Michael_Cox "Bryan-Michael Cox"), Jason Perry, [Johntá Austin](/wiki/Johnt%C3%A1_Austin "Johntá Austin") & [Mary J. Blige](/wiki/Mary_J._Blige "Mary J. Blige") (2007)
  * "[No One](/wiki/No_One_\(Alicia_Keys_song\) "No One \(Alicia Keys song\)")" – [Alicia Keys](/wiki/Alicia_Keys "Alicia Keys"), [George M. Harry](/wiki/DJ_Dirty_Harry "DJ Dirty Harry") & [Kerry "Krucial" Brothers](/wiki/Kerry_Brothers_Jr. "Kerry Brothers Jr.") (2008)

  *[POTUS  
No.]: Number
  *[b.]: born
  *[q.v.]: see also
  *[25]: Not actually given in Africanus; the number is deduced from Manetho's other copyists, as well as Africanus' own sum of years.
  *[69 years]: The correct sum should be 97; the total excluding Queen Hatshepsut is 75 years.
  *[263 years]: This is actually the sum of the first 13 kings, which amounts to 262 years. Adding the reign of the 14th king raises the number to 267 years.
  *[348 years]: The sum of the first thirteen kings is 263 years, just as Africanus. The "correct" sum of years would be 376.
  *[41 years]: Africanus gives 46 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[35 years]: Africanus gives 14 years, but this does not match the stated total.
  *[ 120 years]: Africanus' sum of items comes to 130 years.
  *[40 years]: Eusebius gives 25 years
  *[8 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[ 40 years]: Eusebius gives 44 years
  *[14 years]: Eusebius gives 12 years
  *[18 years]: Eusebius gives 20 years
